- **model-everything**: Use when a document claims something checkable about code: API names, paths, enums, feasibility, protocol behaviour, timing, 'always/never'. Enumerates the claims and verifies each with file:line evidence, then builds an executable model when behaviour or safety is at stake. Siblings: model-everything-diagram, -uml, -design, -interleaving, -datamodel, -structure. ([AmethystLuna/dsh-model-everything](https://github.com/AmethystLuna/dsh-model-everything/tree/HEAD/skills/model-everything))
- **model-everything-datamodel**: Use when reviewing a schema, a migration or a data guarantee: entities, fields, constraints, uniqueness, referential integrity, data invariants, 'no orphan rows', and before/after equivalence. It runs the data model as a model: key and domain checks, migration coverage, copy consistency, rollback symmetry, and whether a before/after pair actually preserved the data. State machines: model-everything; module dependencies: model-everything-structure; diagrams: model-everything-uml. ([AmethystLuna/dsh-model-everything](https://github.com/AmethystLuna/dsh-model-everything/tree/HEAD/skills/model-everything-datamodel))
- **model-everything-design**: Use when a design has to keep its own declarations: does the layering hold, do responsibilities match the dependencies they own, can a subtype stand in for its supertype, is an interface wider than its callers. Also reports the graph's shape (cycles and the fewest edges to break them, load-bearing elements, ranks, fan-in/out) without judging the design. A drawing of an architecture audited against a matrix is model-everything-structure. Doctrine: the model-everything skill. ([AmethystLuna/dsh-model-everything](https://github.com/AmethystLuna/dsh-model-everything/tree/HEAD/skills/model-everything-design))
- **model-everything-diagram**: Use when drawing a diagram from its source text, showing one to a reader, or checking its structure: Mermaid and PlantUML subsets, structure budgets, and containment diffs. Reach for it when an explanation is clearer shown than told - a layout, a flow, a change - and put the picture beside the explanation. Every line it cannot draw is refused by number. The drawing proves layout, not design. Doctrine: the model-everything skill. ([AmethystLuna/dsh-model-everything](https://github.com/AmethystLuna/dsh-model-everything/tree/HEAD/skills/model-everything-diagram))
- **model-everything-interleaving**: Use when a design or plan claims concurrency, synchrony or async safety, or names concurrent writers, mutexes, interrupts, async tasks, promises or an event loop. It mines the claims (thread-safe, lock-free, no data race, ISR-safe, non-blocking) and routes each to dedicated verification (TSan, Helgrind, CBMC, TLA+, UPPAAL); it does not prove safety. Doctrine: the model-everything skill. ([AmethystLuna/dsh-model-everything](https://github.com/AmethystLuna/dsh-model-everything/tree/HEAD/skills/model-everything-interleaving))
- **model-everything-report-reading**: Use when reading a report this plugin returned: what does the verdict mean, why did it come out this way, which field do I act on, and what does the report not prove. It gives the reading order — did it run, what did it decide, what next — where each family keeps its findings, and the two readings that are easy to get backwards. It reads reports; it does not produce or check models. Producing one is model-everything. ([AmethystLuna/dsh-model-everything](https://github.com/AmethystLuna/dsh-model-everything/tree/HEAD/skills/model-everything-report-reading))
- **model-everything-structure**: Use when auditing architecture or module structure from a drawing. It reads component, package, class, object or deployment diagrams as a dependency graph and checks isolated nodes, dangling endpoints, cycles, allowed-dependency violations, layer violations and missing required edges, across one level or several. Audits the drawing, not the code — reconcile it with a source-side scan. SRP/LSP/ISP questions are model-everything-design. Doctrine: the model-everything skill. ([AmethystLuna/dsh-model-everything](https://github.com/AmethystLuna/dsh-model-everything/tree/HEAD/skills/model-everything-structure))
- **model-everything-uml**: Use when drawing a UML diagram or auditing a hand-drawn one: parse it as a state, activity, timing, profile or composite structure model, render a model in a notation that can draw it, and check round-trip fidelity. A component, class, object, package or deployment diagram is read as a dependency graph by the model-everything-structure skill; an entity-relationship diagram is not a UML family and is refused, with the reason. Doctrine: the model-everything skill. ([AmethystLuna/dsh-model-everything](https://github.com/AmethystLuna/dsh-model-everything/tree/HEAD/skills/model-everything-uml))
